- **feature-library-scout**: Research and recommend vetted libraries per feature problem (lists, camera, forms, keyboard, sheets), ranked by adoption signal, writing FEATURE_LIBRARIES.md from captured REFERENCES.md sources. Use when the stack is chosen and you want per-feature libraries vetted. Do not use to pick stack layers (use stack-recommend), check framework patterns (use stack-currency-check), synthesize captured sources (use external-research), or without a task (run task-init first). For more than 3 feature problems needing a deep sweep, use feature-library-scout-fleet. ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/feature-library-scout.md) · ★10)
- **feature-library-scout-fleet**: Orchestrator-workers variant of feature-library-scout for deep per-feature-problem library research. One worker per problem ranks libraries by adoption signal. Use when over 3 distinct feature problems each need a deep read. Do not use for 1 to 3 problems (use feature-library-scout), stack layers (use stack-recommend), framework pattern currency (use stack-currency-check), or with no task folder (run task-init). ([Mozurok/fhorja.dev](https://github.com/Mozurok/fhorja.dev/blob/HEAD/commands/feature-library-scout-fleet.md) · ★10)
